## Look at the Margin First

Turn to Psalm 119 in your own Bible and look down the left-hand side.

Above verse 1 you will find **ALEPH**. Above verse 9, **BETH**. Above verse 105 — the verse you already know by heart — **NUN**. Keep going and you will count twenty-two of them, ending at **TAU** above verse 169.

Those are not a commentator's additions. They are printed in the King James text itself, and they are the skeleton of the poem.

Psalm 119 has **176 verses arranged in twenty-two stanzas of eight**. In Hebrew, every verse of a stanza begins with that stanza's letter — eight verses starting with *aleph*, then eight with *beth*, straight through to *tav*. Twenty-two stanzas, because the Hebrew alphabet has twenty-two letters. A poem built this way is called an **acrostic**.

The longest chapter in scripture is shaped like an alphabet chart, and the translators left the letter names in so you can see it without reading a word of Hebrew.

---

## How to Use This Map

Below are all twenty-two letters. Each card holds four things:

- **The letter itself** — its name, how it sounds, its numeric value, and what it means as a Hebrew word.
- **What the ancient sign shows.** These letters began as pictures of ordinary things: an ox, a house, a door, a hand, water, an eye. The oldest examples we have found were scratched into rock in the Sinai around the eighteenth century before Christ — though that is where the earliest surviving inscriptions turned up, not necessarily where the alphabet began. For most letters the picture and the name plainly agree; **for a few the origin is genuinely debated among scholars, and the table gives the leading proposal rather than a settled fact.**
- **The journey of the shape** — Egyptian, Proto-Sinaitic, Phoenician, Aramaic, Hebrew, Greek, Latin. Read that row left to right and you will watch an ox head become the letter **A**.

**Where the alphabet came from.** Scholars generally hold that these letters were **adapted from Egyptian hieroglyphs** — a picture-sign was borrowed, and instead of standing for a word it was made to stand for the *first sound* of that word's name. An ox head, *aleph*, came to mean the sound at the start of *aleph*. That is why the first column of every row below is Egyptian.

And **Proto-Sinaitic — the second column — is the earliest alphabet known to us.** Writing itself is much older; Egypt and Mesopotamia had been writing for well over a thousand years. But those systems needed hundreds of signs. **An alphabet needs about two dozen**, because each sign carries a sound rather than a whole word, and every alphabet in use today — Hebrew, Greek, Latin, Cyrillic, Arabic — descends from that one idea. **The alphabet Psalm 119 is built on is the ancestor of the one you are reading right now.**

**A caution about where it began, because it is easy to state too confidently.** The Sinai inscriptions are the earliest examples anyone has *found*. That is not the same as the place the alphabet was *invented*. The letter names are **Northwest Semitic** — the language family of Hebrew, Phoenician and Aramaic, spoken across Canaan and the Levant — so the alphabet may well have taken shape somewhere in that world and simply not survived anywhere we have dug. Stone in a desert mining camp lasts; ink on leather in a Canaanite town does not. **What we can say is where the earliest surviving evidence is. Where it started is a separate question, and an open one.**
- **Its eight verses of Psalm 119**, behind a dropdown. Open it and you will find the Hebrew with **the stanza's opening word marked in orange**, the King James translation, and a link straight into the lexicon entry for that word.

**A note on the opening words.** An acrostic constrains the first *letter* of a line — it does not require the first *word* to mean what the letter's name means. So this is not a puzzle where each stanza should "match" its letter. Where an opening word does reach for the letter's own vocabulary it is worth noticing, and where it does not, the stanza is simply doing what the poem asked of it.

Two are worth watching for as you explore — and the criterion is a narrow one: **the stanza's opening word is itself the letter's own word.** That is a lexical match, one lens among several, and it is not the only way a stanza and its letter meet. **Yod** (verse 73) opens *"Thy hands have made me"* — and *yod* is a hand; the word is the letter's own word. **Gimel** (verse 17) opens *"Deal bountifully with thy servant"*, from the root that also gives "camel." And in the **Lamed** stanza, verses 94 and 95 open with the letter *lamed* standing alone as a preposition — the letter is the word.

format('truetype'); }
.lm-wrap{--lm-accent:var(--sage-dark,#4a6b52);--lm-gold:var(--gold,#d5a93c);
  --lm-terra:var(--terracotta,#c65528);--lm-slate:var(--slate,#57899c);
  --lm-border:var(--border,#c6d4c8);--lm-muted:var(--text-muted,#718096);
  --lm-body:var(--text-secondary,#4a5568);--lm-bg:var(--sage-bg,#f2f5f2);}
.lm-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(320px,1fr));gap:18px;margin:28px 0}
.lm-card{border:1px solid var(--lm-border);border-radius:var(--radius-lg,12px);background:#fff;
  padding:18px 18px 14px;box-shadow:var(--shadow-sm,0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,.08));
  transition:box-shadow .18s ease,transform .18s ease}
.lm-card:hover{box-shadow:var(--shadow-md,0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.1));transform:translateY(-2px)}
.lm-head{display:flex;gap:14px;align-items:center;margin-bottom:12px}
.lm-glyph{font-family:"Frank Ruhl Libre","Frank Ruehl CLM","Times New Roman",serif;font-size:2.9em;
  line-height:1;color:var(--lm-accent);flex:none;min-width:1.4em;text-align:center}
.lm-id h3{margin:0;font-size:var(--text-md,1.1em);color:var(--text-primary,#2d3748)}
.lm-pron{font-weight:400;color:var(--lm-muted);font-size:.82em}
.lm-meta{font-size:var(--text-xs,.75em);color:var(--lm-muted);margin-top:3px;
  font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.lm-def{font-size:var(--text-sm,.85em);color:var(--lm-body);margin:0 0 12px;line-height:1.6}
.lm-sign,.lm-gram,.lm-theme{font-size:var(--text-sm,.85em);line-height:1.55;padding:8px 11px;
  border-radius:6px;margin:0 0 9px;background:var(--lm-bg)}
.lm-sign{border-left:3px solid var(--lm-terra)}
.lm-gram{border-left:3px solid var(--lm-slate)}
.lm-theme{border-left:3px solid var(--lm-gold);font-style:italic}
.lm-glab,.lm-wlab{display:block;font-size:.68em;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;
  color:var(--lm-muted);margin-bottom:3px;font-weight:600;font-style:normal}
.lm-chain{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(7,1fr);gap:3px;margin:12px 0 10px}
.lm-chain span{text-align:center;padding:5px 2px;border:1px solid var(--lm-border);border-radius:4px;
  background:#fff;min-width:0}
.lm-chain i{display:block;font-size:1.15em;line-height:1.3;font-style:normal;
  overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:var(--lm-accent)}
.lm-chain u{display:block;font-size:.5em;color:var(--lm-muted);text-decoration:none;line-height:1.25;
  overflow-wrap:anywhere;font-style:italic;min-height:.7em}
.lm-chain em{display:block;font-size:.46em;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.02em;
  color:var(--lm-muted);font-style:normal;margin-top:2px}
.lm-ps{font-family:'PS',serif}
.lm-anc{font-family:"Noto Sans Phoenician","Noto Sans Imperial Aramaic",
  "Noto Sans Egyptian Hieroglyphs","Segoe UI Historic",serif}
.lm-heb-g{font-family:"Frank Ruhl Libre","Frank Ruehl CLM","Times New Roman",serif}
.lm-drop{margin-top:10px;border-top:1px solid var(--lm-border);padding-top:9px}
.lm-drop>summary{cursor:pointer;font-size:var(--text-sm,.85em);font-weight:600;
  color:var(--lm-accent);list-style:none;padding:4px 0;user-select:none}
.lm-drop>summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none}
.lm-drop>summary::before{content:"▸";display:inline-block;margin-right:7px;
  transition:transform .18s ease;color:var(--lm-gold)}
.lm-drop[open]>summary::before{transform:rotate(90deg)}
.lm-drop>summary:hover{color:var(--lm-terra)}
.lm-verses{margin-top:10px}
.lm-v{display:grid;grid-template-columns:2.1em 1fr;gap:9px;padding:9px 0;
  border-top:1px solid #eef2ee}
.lm-v:first-child{border-top:0}
.lm-vnum a{display:inline-block;font-size:.72em;font-weight:700;color:var(--lm-muted);
  text-decoration:none;background:var(--lm-bg);border-radius:4px;padding:2px 5px;
  font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.lm-vnum a:hover{color:#fff;background:var(--lm-accent)}
.lm-heb{font-family:"Frank Ruhl Libre","Frank Ruehl CLM","Times New Roman",serif;font-size:1.28em;
  line-height:1.95;color:var(--text-primary,#2d3748);margin-bottom:3px}
.lm-acro{color:var(--lm-terra);font-weight:700;
  background:linear-gradient(transparent 58%,var(--gold-pale,#f5ecd0) 58%);padding:0 .12em;
  border-radius:2px}
.lm-kjv{font-size:var(--text-sm,.85em);color:var(--lm-body);line-height:1.6}
.lm-word{font-size:.78em;color:var(--lm-muted);margin-top:5px}
.lm-word .lm-wlab{display:inline;margin:0 5px 0 0;font-size:.9em}
.lm-nolex{font-style:italic}
.lm-legend{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:16px;padding:12px 14px;border:1px solid var(--lm-border);
  border-radius:var(--radius,8px);background:var(--lm-bg);font-size:var(--text-sm,.85em);margin:20px 0}
.lm-legend div{display:flex;gap:7px;align-items:baseline}
.lm-sw{width:11px;height:11px;border-radius:2px;display:inline-block;flex:none;position:relative;top:1px}
@media (max-width:640px){.lm-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}
  .lm-chain{grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr)}}
</style>

<div class="lm-wrap">

<div class="lm-legend">
<div><span class="lm-sw" style="background:var(--terracotta,#c65528)"></span> what the ancient sign shows</div>
<div><span class="lm-sw" style="background:var(--slate,#57899c)"></span> how the letter is used in Hebrew</div>
<div><span class="lm-sw" style="background:var(--gold,#d5a93c)"></span> the theme of its Psalm 119 stanza</div>
</div>

<div class="lm-grid">
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-1">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">א</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Aleph <span class="lm-pron">ʾ (glottal stop)</span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">1 of 22 &middot; numeric value 1 &middot; Psalm 119:1&ndash;8</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">First letter of the Hebrew alphabet, numeric value 1. The ancient form depicts an ox head. Not a vowel — it carries a glottal stop, the catch in the throat before a vowel.</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>an ox head</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>As a verb prefix, marks first person singular in the imperfect — I will… . One of the four letters (א ה ו י) that can stand for a vowel instead of a consonant.</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓃾</i><u>ox head</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">a</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤀</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡀</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">א</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Α</i><u></u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Aa</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:1&ndash;8 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/1/" target="_blank">1</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">אַשְׁרֵ֥י</span> תְמִֽימֵי דָ֑רֶךְ הַֽ֝הֹלְכִ֗ים בְּתוֹרַ֥ת יְהוָֽה</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Blessed are the undefiled in the way, who walk in the law of the LORD.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h835/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>'ešer</em></a> (אַשְׁרֵ֥י) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h835/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H835</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/2/" target="_blank">2</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">אַ֭שְׁרֵי</span> נֹצְרֵ֥י עֵדֹתָ֗יו בְּכָל לֵ֥ב יִדְרְשֽׁוּהוּ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Blessed are they that keep his testimonies, and that seek him with the whole heart.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h835/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>'ešer</em></a> (אַ֭שְׁרֵי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h835/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H835</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/3/" target="_blank">3</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">אַ֭ף</span> לֹֽא פָעֲל֣וּ עַוְלָ֑ה בִּדְרָכָ֥יו הָלָֽכוּ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">They also do no iniquity: they walk in his ways.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h637/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>'ap̄</em></a> (אַ֭ף) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h637/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H637</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/4/" target="_blank">4</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">אַ֭תָּה</span> צִוִּ֥יתָה פִקֻּדֶ֗יךָ לִשְׁמֹ֥ר מְאֹֽד</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thou hast commanded us to keep thy precepts diligently.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h859/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>'atâ</em></a> (אַ֭תָּה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h859/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H859</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/5/" target="_blank">5</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">אַ֭חֲלַי</span> יִכֹּ֥נוּ דְרָכָ֗י לִשְׁמֹ֥ר חֻקֶּֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">O that my ways were directed to keep thy statutes!</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h305/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>'aḥălay</em></a> (אַ֭חֲלַי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h305/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H305</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/6/" target="_blank">6</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">אָ֥ז</span> לֹא אֵב֑וֹשׁ בְּ֝הַבִּיטִ֗י אֶל כָּל מִצְוֺתֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Then shall I not be ashamed, when I have respect unto all thy commandments.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h227/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>'āz</em></a> (אָ֥ז) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h227/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H227</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/7/" target="_blank">7</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">א֭וֹדְךָ</span> בְּיֹ֣שֶׁר לֵבָ֑ב בְּ֝לָמְדִ֗י מִשְׁפְּטֵ֥י צִדְקֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I will praise thee with uprightness of heart, when I shall have learned thy righteous judgments.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3034/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>yāḏâ</em></a> (א֭וֹדְךָ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3034/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H3034</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/8/" target="_blank">8</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">אֶת</span> חֻקֶּ֥יךָ אֶשְׁמֹ֑ר אַֽל תַּעַזְבֵ֥נִי עַד מְאֹֽד</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I will keep thy statutes: O forsake me not utterly.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h853/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>'ēṯ</em></a> (אֶת) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h853/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H853</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</details>
</article>
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-2">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">ב</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Bet, Vet <span class="lm-pron">b, b(v)</span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">2 of 22 &middot; numeric value 2 &middot; Psalm 119:9&ndash;16</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">Second letter, value 2. The name means house , and the ancient form is a floor-plan of a house or tent. Sounds b ; without the inner dot, v .</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>the floor-plan of a house or tent</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>Prefixed בְּ — in, with, by, among . One of the six begadkephat letters that soften when they lose the inner dot.</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓉐</i><u>house plan</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">b</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤁</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡁</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">ב</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Β</i><u></u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Bb</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:9&ndash;16 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/9/" target="_blank">9</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">בַּמֶּ֣ה</span> יְזַכֶּה נַּ֭עַר אֶת אָרְח֑וֹ לִ֝שְׁמֹ֗ר כִּדְבָרֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Wherewithal shall a young man cleanse his way? by taking heed thereto according to thy word.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h4100/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>mâ</em></a> (בַּמֶּ֣ה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h4100/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H4100</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/10/" target="_blank">10</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">בְּכָל</span> לִבִּ֥י דְרַשְׁתִּ֑יךָ אַל תַּ֝שְׁגֵּ֗נִי מִמִּצְוֺתֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">With my whole heart have I sought thee: O let me not wander from thy commandments.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3605/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>kôl</em></a> (בְּכָל) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3605/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H3605</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/11/" target="_blank">11</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">בְּ֭לִבִּי</span> צָפַ֣נְתִּי אִמְרָתֶ֑ךָ לְ֝מַ֗עַן לֹ֣א אֶֽחֱטָא לָֽךְ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thy word have I hid in mine heart, that I might not sin against thee.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3820/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>lēḇ</em></a> (בְּ֭לִבִּי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3820/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H3820</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/12/" target="_blank">12</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">בָּר֖וּךְ</span> אַתָּ֥ה יְהוָ֗ה לַמְּדֵ֥נִי חֻקֶּֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Blessed art thou, O LORD: teach me thy statutes.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1288/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>bāraḵ</em></a> (בָּר֖וּךְ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1288/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1288</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/13/" target="_blank">13</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">בִּשְׂפָתַ֥י</span> סִפַּ֑רְתִּי כֹּ֝֗ל מִשְׁפְּטֵי פִֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">With my lips have I declared all the judgments of thy mouth.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h8193/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>śāp̄â</em></a> (בִּשְׂפָתַ֥י) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h8193/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H8193</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/14/" target="_blank">14</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">בְּדֶ֖רֶךְ</span> עֵדְוֺתֶ֥יךָ שַׂ֗שְׂתִּי כְּעַ֣ל כָּל הֽוֹן</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I have rejoiced in the way of thy testimonies, as much as in all riches.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1870/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>dereḵ</em></a> (בְּדֶ֖רֶךְ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1870/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1870</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/15/" target="_blank">15</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">בְּפִקֻּדֶ֥יךָ</span> אָשִׂ֑יחָה וְ֝אַבִּ֗יטָה אֹרְחֹתֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I will meditate in thy precepts, and have respect unto thy ways.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6490/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>piqqûḏîm</em></a> (בְּפִקֻּדֶ֥יךָ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6490/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6490</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/16/" target="_blank">16</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">בְּחֻקֹּתֶ֥יךָ</span> אֶֽשְׁתַּעֲשָׁ֑ע לֹ֭א אֶשְׁכַּ֣ח דְּבָרֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I will delight myself in thy statutes: I will not forget thy word.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2708/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ḥuqqâ</em></a> (בְּחֻקֹּתֶ֥יךָ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2708/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2708</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</details>
</article>
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-3">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">ג</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Gimel <span class="lm-pron">g</span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">3 of 22 &middot; numeric value 3 &middot; Psalm 119:17&ndash;24</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">Third letter, value 3. Sounds g . The origin is contested: the name is traditionally linked to gamal , camel, while the oldest sign is read by some as a throw stick and by others as a ring stand for water jars (Egyptian W011).</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>a throw stick, a camel, or a stand that holds a water jar — the three readings compete</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>One of the six begadkephat letters. No common prefix use.</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓌙 𓎼</i><u>throw stick / jar stand</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">g</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤂</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡂</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">ג</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Γ</i><u></u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Cc, Gg</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:17&ndash;24 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/17/" target="_blank">17</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">גְּמֹ֖ל</span> עַֽל עַבְדְּךָ֥ אֶֽחְיֶ֗ה וְאֶשְׁמְרָ֥ה דְבָרֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Deal bountifully with thy servant, that I may live, and keep thy word.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> (גְּמֹ֖ל) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1580/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1580</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/18/" target="_blank">18</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">גַּל</span> עֵינַ֥י וְאַבִּ֑יטָה נִ֝פְלָא֗וֹת מִתּוֹרָתֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Open thou mine eyes, that I may behold wondrous things out of thy law.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1540/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>gālâ</em></a> (גַּל) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1540/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1540</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/19/" target="_blank">19</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">גֵּ֣ר</span> אָנֹכִ֣י בָאָ֑רֶץ אַל תַּסְתֵּ֥ר מִ֝מֶּ֗נִּי מִצְוֺתֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I am a stranger in the earth: hide not thy commandments from me.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1616/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>gār</em></a> (גֵּ֣ר) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1616/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1616</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/20/" target="_blank">20</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">גָּרְסָ֣ה</span> נַפְשִׁ֣י לְתַאֲבָ֑ה אֶֽל מִשְׁפָּטֶ֥יךָ בְכָל עֵֽת</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">My soul breaketh for the longing that it hath unto thy judgments at all times.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1638/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>gāras</em></a> (גָּרְסָ֣ה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1638/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1638</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/21/" target="_blank">21</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">גָּ֭עַרְתָּ</span> זֵדִ֣ים אֲרוּרִ֑ים הַ֝שֹּׁגִים מִמִּצְוֺתֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thou hast rebuked the proud that are cursed, which do err from thy commandments.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1605/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>gāʿar</em></a> (גָּ֭עַרְתָּ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1605/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1605</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/22/" target="_blank">22</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">גַּ֣ל</span> מֵֽ֭עָלַי חֶרְפָּ֣ה וָב֑וּז כִּ֖י עֵדֹתֶ֣יךָ נָצָֽרְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Remove from me reproach and contempt; for I have kept thy testimonies.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> (גַּ֣ל) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1556/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1556</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/23/" target="_blank">23</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">גַּ֤ם</span> יָֽשְׁב֣וּ שָׂ֭רִים בִּ֣י נִדְבָּ֑רוּ עַ֝בְדְּךָ֗ יָשִׂ֥יחַ בְּחֻקֶּֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Princes also did sit and speak against me: but thy servant did meditate in thy statutes.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> (גַּ֤ם) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1571/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1571</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/24/" target="_blank">24</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">גַּֽם</span> עֵ֭דֹתֶיךָ שַׁעֲשֻׁעָ֗י אַנְשֵׁ֥י עֲצָתִֽי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thy testimonies also are my delight and my counsellors.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> (גַּֽם) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1571/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1571</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</details>
</article>
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-4">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">ד</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Daleth <span class="lm-pron">d, d(th)</span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">4 of 22 &middot; numeric value 4 &middot; Psalm 119:25&ndash;32</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">Fourth letter, value 4. The name means door , and the ancient form draws one. Sounds d .</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>a door</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>One of the six begadkephat letters. In Aramaic, prefixed דְּ marks the relative — which, of .</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓇯</i><u>door</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">d</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤃</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡃</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">ד</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Δ</i><u></u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Dd</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:25&ndash;32 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/25/" target="_blank">25</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">דָּֽבְקָ֣ה</span> לֶעָפָ֣ר נַפְשִׁ֑י חַ֝יֵּ֗נִי כִּדְבָרֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">My soul cleaveth unto the dust: quicken thou me according to thy word.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1692/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>dāḇaq</em></a> (דָּֽבְקָ֣ה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1692/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1692</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/26/" target="_blank">26</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">דְּרָכַ֣י</span> סִ֭פַּרְתִּי וַֽתַּעֲנֵ֗נִי לַמְּדֵ֥נִי חֻקֶּֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I have declared my ways, and thou heardest me: teach me thy statutes.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1870/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>dereḵ</em></a> (דְּרָכַ֣י) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1870/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1870</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/27/" target="_blank">27</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">דֶּֽרֶךְ</span> פִּקּוּדֶ֥יךָ הֲבִינֵ֑נִי וְ֝אָשִׂ֗יחָה בְּנִפְלְאוֹתֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Make me to understand the way of thy precepts: so shall I talk of thy wondrous works.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1870/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>dereḵ</em></a> (דֶּֽרֶךְ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1870/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1870</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/28/" target="_blank">28</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">דָּלְפָ֣ה</span> נַ֭פְשִׁי מִתּוּגָ֑ה קַ֝יְּמֵ֗נִי כִּדְבָרֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">My soul melteth for heaviness: strengthen thou me according unto thy word.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1811/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>dālap̄</em></a> (דָּלְפָ֣ה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1811/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1811</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/29/" target="_blank">29</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">דֶּֽרֶךְ</span> שֶׁ֭קֶר הָסֵ֣ר מִמֶּ֑נִּי וְֽתוֹרָתְךָ֥ חָנֵּֽנִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Remove from me the way of lying: and grant me thy law graciously.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1870/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>dereḵ</em></a> (דֶּֽרֶךְ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1870/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1870</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/30/" target="_blank">30</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">דֶּֽרֶךְ</span> אֱמוּנָ֥ה בָחָ֑רְתִּי מִשְׁפָּטֶ֥יךָ שִׁוִּֽיתִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I have chosen the way of truth: thy judgments have I laid before me.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1870/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>dereḵ</em></a> (דֶּֽרֶךְ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1870/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1870</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/31/" target="_blank">31</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">דָּבַ֥קְתִּי</span> בְעֵֽדְוֺתֶ֑יךָ יְ֝הוָ֗ה אַל תְּבִישֵֽׁנִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I have stuck unto thy testimonies: O LORD, put me not to shame.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1692/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>dāḇaq</em></a> (דָּבַ֥קְתִּי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1692/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1692</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/32/" target="_blank">32</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">דֶּֽרֶךְ</span> מִצְוֺתֶ֥יךָ אָר֑וּץ כִּ֖י תַרְחִ֣יב לִבִּֽי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I will run the way of thy commandments, when thou shalt enlarge my heart.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1870/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>dereḵ</em></a> (דֶּֽרֶךְ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1870/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1870</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</details>
</article>
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-5">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">ה</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Hey <span class="lm-pron">h, (h,? or silent)</span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">5 of 22 &middot; numeric value 5 &middot; Psalm 119:33&ndash;40</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">Fifth letter, value 5. The ancient form shows a figure with raised arms; the name is usually glossed behold or window . Sounds h , and is often silent at the end of a word. Prefixed to a noun, it is the definite article — the .</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>a figure with raised arms; a window</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>Prefixed הַ — the , the definite article. Also the question marker, turning a statement into a question. Suffixed, the common feminine ending, and the directional toward . One of the four vowel-letters.</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓀠</i><u>jubilation/raised arms</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">h</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤄</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡄</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">ה</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Ε</i><u></u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Ee</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:33&ndash;40 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/33/" target="_blank">33</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">הוֹרֵ֣נִי</span> יְ֭הוָה דֶּ֥רֶךְ חֻקֶּ֗יךָ וְאֶצְּרֶ֥נָּה עֵֽקֶב</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Teach me, O LORD, the way of thy statutes; and I shall keep it unto the end.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3384/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>yārâ</em></a> (הוֹרֵ֣נִי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3384/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H3384</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/34/" target="_blank">34</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">הֲ֭בִינֵנִי</span> וְאֶצְּרָ֥ה תֽוֹרָתֶ֗ךָ וְאֶשְׁמְרֶ֥נָּה בְכָל לֵֽב</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Give me understanding, and I shall keep thy law; yea, I shall observe it with my whole heart.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h995/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>bîn</em></a> (הֲ֭בִינֵנִי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h995/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H995</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/35/" target="_blank">35</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">הַ֭דְרִיכֵנִי</span> בִּנְתִ֣יב מִצְוֺתֶ֑יךָ כִּי ב֥וֹ חָפָֽצְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Make me to go in the path of thy commandments; for therein do I delight.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1869/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>dāraḵ</em></a> (הַ֭דְרִיכֵנִי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1869/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1869</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/36/" target="_blank">36</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">הַט</span> לִ֭בִּי אֶל עֵדְוֺתֶ֗יךָ וְאַ֣ל אֶל בָּֽצַע</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Incline my heart unto thy testimonies, and not to covetousness.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5186/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>nāṭâ</em></a> (הַט) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5186/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5186</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/37/" target="_blank">37</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">הַעֲבֵ֣ר</span> עֵ֭ינַי מֵרְא֣וֹת שָׁ֑וְא בִּדְרָכֶ֥ךָ חַיֵּֽנִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Turn away mine eyes from beholding vanity; and quicken thou me in thy way.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5674/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ʿāḇar</em></a> (הַעֲבֵ֣ר) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5674/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5674</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/38/" target="_blank">38</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">הָקֵ֣ם</span> לְ֭עַבְדְּךָ אִמְרָתֶ֑ךָ אֲ֝שֶׁ֗ר לְיִרְאָתֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Stablish thy word unto thy servant, who is devoted to thy fear.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6965/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>qûm</em></a> (הָקֵ֣ם) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6965/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6965</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/39/" target="_blank">39</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">הַעֲבֵ֣ר</span> חֶ֭רְפָּתִי אֲשֶׁ֣ר יָגֹ֑רְתִּי כִּ֖י מִשְׁפָּטֶ֣יךָ טוֹבִֽים</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Turn away my reproach which I fear: for thy judgments are good.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5674/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ʿāḇar</em></a> (הַעֲבֵ֣ר) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5674/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5674</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/40/" target="_blank">40</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">הִ֭נֵּה</span> תָּאַ֣בְתִּי לְפִקֻּדֶ֑יךָ בְּצִדְקָתְךָ֥ חַיֵּֽנִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Behold, I have longed after thy precepts: quicken me in thy righteousness.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2009/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>hinnê</em></a> (הִ֭נֵּה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2009/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2009</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</details>
</article>
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-6">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">ו</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Vav <span class="lm-pron">w, v</span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">6 of 22 &middot; numeric value 6/400 &middot; Psalm 119:41&ndash;48</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">Sixth letter, value 6. The name means hook or tent peg . Prefixed to a word it is the conjunction and — the commonest use of any Hebrew letter. Also marks the vowels o and u .</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>a tent peg, hook or nail</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>Prefixed וְ — and , the commonest prefix in the Bible. Also the vav-consecutive , which flips a verb&#x27;s tense in narrative — the engine of biblical storytelling. Marks the vowels o and u .</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓍢</i><u>mace/peg</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">w</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤅</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡅</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">ו</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Υ Ϝ</i><u>Digamma</u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Ff, Vv, Uu, Ww</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:41&ndash;48 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/41/" target="_blank">41</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">וִֽיבֹאֻ֣נִי</span> חֲסָדֶ֣ךָ יְהוָ֑ה תְּ֝שֽׁוּעָתְךָ֗ כְּאִמְרָתֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Let thy mercies come also unto me, O LORD, even thy salvation, according to thy word.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h935/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>bô'</em></a> (וִֽיבֹאֻ֣נִי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h935/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H935</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/42/" target="_blank">42</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">וְאֶֽעֱנֶ֣ה</span> חֹרְפִ֣י דָבָ֑ר כִּֽי בָ֝טַחְתִּי בִּדְבָרֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">So shall I have wherewith to answer him that reproacheth me: for I trust in thy word.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6030/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ʿānâ</em></a> (וְאֶֽעֱנֶ֣ה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6030/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6030</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/43/" target="_blank">43</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">וְֽאַל</span> תַּצֵּ֬ל מִפִּ֣י דְבַר אֱמֶ֣ת עַד מְאֹ֑ד כִּ֖י לְמִשְׁפָּטֶ֣ךָ יִחָֽלְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">And take not the word of truth utterly out of my mouth; for I have hoped in thy judgments.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h408/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>'al</em></a> (וְֽאַל) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h408/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H408</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/44/" target="_blank">44</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">וְאֶשְׁמְרָ֖ה</span> תוֹרָתְךָ֥ תָמִ֗יד לְעוֹלָ֥ם וָעֶֽד</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">So shall I keep thy law continually for ever and ever.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h8104/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>šāmar</em></a> (וְאֶשְׁמְרָ֖ה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h8104/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H8104</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/45/" target="_blank">45</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">וְאֶתְהַלְּכָ֥ה</span> בָרְחָבָ֑ה כִּ֖י פִקֻּדֶ֣יךָ דָרָֽשְׁתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">And I will walk at liberty: for I seek thy precepts.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1980/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>hālaḵ</em></a> (וְאֶתְהַלְּכָ֥ה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1980/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1980</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/46/" target="_blank">46</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">וַאֲדַבְּרָ֣ה</span> בְ֭עֵדֹתֶיךָ נֶ֥גֶד מְלָכִ֗ים וְלֹ֣א אֵבֽוֹשׁ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I will speak of thy testimonies also before kings, and will not be ashamed.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1696/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>dāḇar</em></a> (וַאֲדַבְּרָ֣ה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1696/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1696</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/47/" target="_blank">47</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">וְאֶשְׁתַּֽעֲשַׁ֥ע</span> בְּמִצְוֺתֶ֗יךָ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אָהָֽבְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">And I will delight myself in thy commandments, which I have loved.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h8173/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>šāʿaʿ</em></a> (וְאֶשְׁתַּֽעֲשַׁ֥ע) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h8173/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H8173</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/48/" target="_blank">48</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">וְאֶשָּֽׂא</span> כַפַּ֗י אֶֽל מִ֭צְוֺתֶיךָ אֲשֶׁ֥ר אָהָ֗בְתִּי וְאָשִׂ֥יחָה בְחֻקֶּֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">My hands also will I lift up unto thy commandments, which I have loved; and I will meditate in thy statutes.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5375/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>nāśā'</em></a> (וְאֶשָּֽׂא) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5375/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5375</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</details>
</article>
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-7">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">ז</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Zayin <span class="lm-pron">z</span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">7 of 22 &middot; numeric value 7 &middot; Psalm 119:49&ndash;56</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">Seventh letter, value 7. The name is linked to weapon ; the ancient form is a mattock or blade. Sounds z .</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>a mattock or plow as readily as a sword — a tool of planting and nourishment, not only a weapon</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>No common prefix or grammatical function — it appears only as a root letter.</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓍁</i><u>weapon</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">z</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤆</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡆</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">ז</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Ζ</i><u></u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Zz</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:49&ndash;56 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/49/" target="_blank">49</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">זְכֹר</span> דָּבָ֥ר לְעַבְדֶּ֑ךָ עַ֝֗ל אֲשֶׁ֣ר יִֽחַלְתָּֽנִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Remember the word unto thy servant, upon which thou hast caused me to hope.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2142/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>zâkar</em></a> (זְכֹר) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2142/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2142</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/50/" target="_blank">50</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">זֹ֣את</span> נֶחָמָתִ֣י בְעָנְיִ֑י כִּ֖י אִמְרָתְךָ֣ חִיָּֽתְנִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">This is my comfort in my affliction: for thy word hath quickened me.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2063/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>zō'ṯ</em></a> (זֹ֣את) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2063/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2063</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/51/" target="_blank">51</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">זֵ֭דִים</span> הֱלִיצֻ֣נִי עַד מְאֹ֑ד מִ֝תּֽוֹרָתְךָ֗ לֹ֣א נָטִֽיתִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">The proud have had me greatly in derision: yet have I not declined from thy law.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2086/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>zēḏ</em></a> (זֵ֭דִים) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2086/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2086</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/52/" target="_blank">52</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">זָ֘כַ֤רְתִּי</span> מִשְׁפָּטֶ֖יךָ מֵעוֹלָ֥ם יְהוָ֗ה וָֽאֶתְנֶחָֽם</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I remembered thy judgments of old, O LORD; and have comforted myself.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2142/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>zâkar</em></a> (זָ֘כַ֤רְתִּי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2142/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2142</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/53/" target="_blank">53</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">זַלְעָפָ֣ה</span> אֲ֭חָזַתְנִי מֵרְשָׁעִ֑ים עֹ֝זְבֵ֗י תּוֹרָתֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Horror hath taken hold upon me because of the wicked that forsake thy law.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2152/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>zalʻâphâh</em></a> (זַלְעָפָ֣ה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2152/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2152</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/54/" target="_blank">54</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">זְ֭מִרוֹת</span> הָֽיוּ לִ֥י חֻקֶּ֗יךָ בְּבֵ֣ית מְגוּרָֽי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thy statutes have been my songs in the house of my pilgrimage.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2158/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>zâmîyr</em></a> (זְ֭מִרוֹת) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2158/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2158</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/55/" target="_blank">55</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">זָ֘כַ֤רְתִּי</span> בַלַּ֣יְלָה שִׁמְךָ֣ יְהוָ֑ה וָֽ֝אֶשְׁמְרָ֗ה תּוֹרָתֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I have remembered thy name, O LORD, in the night, and have kept thy law.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2142/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>zâkar</em></a> (זָ֘כַ֤רְתִּי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2142/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2142</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/56/" target="_blank">56</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">זֹ֥את</span> הָֽיְתָה לִּ֑י כִּ֖י פִקֻּדֶ֣יךָ נָצָֽרְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">This I had, because I kept thy precepts.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2063/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>zō'ṯ</em></a> (זֹ֥את) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2063/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2063</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</details>
</article>
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-8">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">ח</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Chet <span class="lm-pron">h(ch)</span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">8 of 22 &middot; numeric value 8 &middot; Psalm 119:57&ndash;64</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">Eighth letter, value 8. The ancient form is a courtyard wall or fence. A voiceless pharyngeal fricative — the ch of Bach, made deep in the throat.</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>a courtyard wall, fence or ladder</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>No common prefix or grammatical function — it appears only as a root letter.</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓉗</i><u>courtyard/fence</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">x</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤇</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡇</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">ח</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Η</i><u></u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Hh</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:57&ndash;64 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/57/" target="_blank">57</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">חֶלְקִ֖י</span> יְהוָ֥ה אָמַ֗רְתִּי לִשְׁמֹ֥ר דְּבָרֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thou art my portion, O LORD: I have said that I would keep thy words.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2506/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ḥēleq</em></a> (חֶלְקִ֖י) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2506/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2506</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/58/" target="_blank">58</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">חִלִּ֣יתִי</span> פָנֶ֣יךָ בְכָל לֵ֑ב חָ֝נֵּ֗נִי כְּאִמְרָתֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I intreated thy favour with my whole heart: be merciful unto me according to thy word.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2470/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ḥālâ</em></a> (חִלִּ֣יתִי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2470/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2470</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/59/" target="_blank">59</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">חִשַּׁ֥בְתִּי</span> דְרָכָ֑י וָאָשִׁ֥יבָה רַ֝גְלַ֗י אֶל עֵדֹתֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I thought on my ways, and turned my feet unto thy testimonies.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2803/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ḥāšaḇ</em></a> (חִשַּׁ֥בְתִּי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2803/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2803</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/60/" target="_blank">60</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">חַ֭שְׁתִּי</span> וְלֹ֣א הִתְמַהְמָ֑הְתִּי לִ֝שְׁמֹ֗ר מִצְוֺתֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I made haste, and delayed not to keep thy commandments.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2363/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ḥûš</em></a> (חַ֭שְׁתִּי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2363/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2363</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/61/" target="_blank">61</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">חֶבְלֵ֣י</span> רְשָׁעִ֣ים עִוְּדֻ֑נִי תּֽ֝וֹרָתְךָ֗ לֹ֣א שָׁכָֽחְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">The bands of the wicked have robbed me: but I have not forgotten thy law.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2256/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ḥebel</em></a> (חֶבְלֵ֣י) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2256/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2256</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/62/" target="_blank">62</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">חֲצֽוֹת</span> לַ֗יְלָה אָ֭קוּם לְהוֹד֣וֹת לָ֑ךְ עַ֝֗ל מִשְׁפְּטֵ֥י צִדְקֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">At midnight I will rise to give thanks unto thee because of thy righteous judgments.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2676/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ḥāṣôṯ</em></a> (חֲצֽוֹת) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2676/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2676</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/63/" target="_blank">63</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">חָבֵ֣ר</span> אָ֭נִי לְכָל אֲשֶׁ֣ר יְרֵא֑וּךָ וּ֝לְשֹׁמְרֵ֗י פִּקּוּדֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I am a companion of all them that fear thee, and of them that keep thy precepts.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2270/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ḥâbêr</em></a> (חָבֵ֣ר) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2270/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2270</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/64/" target="_blank">64</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">חַסְדְּךָ֣</span> יְ֭הוָה מָלְאָ֥ה הָאָ֗רֶץ חֻקֶּ֥יךָ לַמְּדֵֽנִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">The earth, O LORD, is full of thy mercy: teach me thy statutes.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2617/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ḥeseḏ</em></a> (חַסְדְּךָ֣) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2617/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2617</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</details>
</article>
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-9">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">ט</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Tet <span class="lm-pron">t (t/th)</span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">9 of 22 &middot; numeric value 9 &middot; Psalm 119:65&ndash;72</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">Ninth letter, value 9. The ancient form is a basket or coil. Sounds t , emphatic in older Hebrew.</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>a basket, coil or cup</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>No common prefix or grammatical function — it appears only as a root letter.</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓈎</i><u>basket/wheel</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">j</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤈</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡈</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">ט</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Θ</i><u></u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Tt, Th</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:65&ndash;72 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/65/" target="_blank">65</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">ט֭וֹב</span> עָשִׂ֣יתָ עִֽם עַבְדְּךָ֑ יְ֝הוָ֗ה כִּדְבָרֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thou hast dealt well with thy servant, O LORD, according unto thy word.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2896/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ṭôḇ</em></a> (ט֭וֹב) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2896/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2896</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/66/" target="_blank">66</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">ט֤וּב</span> טַ֣עַם וָדַ֣עַת לַמְּדֵ֑נִי כִּ֖י בְמִצְוֺתֶ֣יךָ הֶאֱמָֽנְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Teach me good judgment and knowledge: for I have believed thy commandments.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2898/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ṭûḇ</em></a> (ט֤וּב) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2898/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2898</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/67/" target="_blank">67</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">טֶ֣רֶם</span> אֶ֭עֱנֶה אֲנִ֣י שֹׁגֵ֑ג וְ֝עַתָּ֗ה אִמְרָתְךָ֥ שָׁמָֽרְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Before I was afflicted I went astray: but now have I kept thy word.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2962/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ṭerem</em></a> (טֶ֣רֶם) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2962/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2962</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/68/" target="_blank">68</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">טוֹב</span> אַתָּ֥ה וּמֵטִ֗יב לַמְּדֵ֥נִי חֻקֶּֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thou art good, and doest good; teach me thy statutes.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2896/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ṭôḇ</em></a> (טוֹב) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2896/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2896</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/69/" target="_blank">69</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">טָפְל֬וּ</span> עָלַ֣י שֶׁ֣קֶר זֵדִ֑ים אֲ֝נִ֗י בְּכָל לֵ֤ב אֱצֹּ֬ר פִּקּוּדֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">The proud have forged a lie against me: but I will keep thy precepts with my whole heart.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2950/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ṭāp̄al</em></a> (טָפְל֬וּ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2950/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2950</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/70/" target="_blank">70</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">טָפַ֣שׁ</span> כַּחֵ֣לֶב לִבָּ֑ם אֲ֝נִ֗י תּוֹרָתְךָ֥ שִֽׁעֲשָֽׁעְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Their heart is as fat as grease; but I delight in thy law.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2954/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ṭāp̄aš</em></a> (טָפַ֣שׁ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2954/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2954</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/71/" target="_blank">71</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">טֽוֹב</span> לִ֥י כִֽי עֻנֵּ֑יתִי לְ֝מַ֗עַן אֶלְמַ֥ד חֻקֶּֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">It is good for me that I have been afflicted; that I might learn thy statutes.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2896/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ṭôḇ</em></a> (טֽוֹב) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2896/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2896</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/72/" target="_blank">72</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">טֽוֹב</span> לִ֥י תֽוֹרַת פִּ֑יךָ מֵ֝אַלְפֵ֗י זָהָ֥ב וָכָֽסֶף</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">The law of thy mouth is better unto me than thousands of gold and silver.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2896/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ṭôḇ</em></a> (טֽוֹב) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2896/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2896</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</details>
</article>
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-10">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">י</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Yod <span class="lm-pron">y (i)</span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">10 of 22 &middot; numeric value 10 &middot; Psalm 119:73&ndash;80</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">Tenth letter, value 10. The name means hand or arm . The smallest letter in the alphabet. Sounds y , and marks the vowel i .</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>a hand or arm</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>Prefixed, marks third person masculine in the imperfect — he will… . Suffixed ִי — my . Also forms gentilic adjectives (Israel → Israeli). One of the four vowel-letters.</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓂝</i><u>arm/hand</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">y</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤉</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡉</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">י</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Ι</i><u></u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Ii, Yy, Jj</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:73&ndash;80 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/73/" target="_blank">73</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">יָדֶ֣יךָ</span> עָ֭שׂוּנִי וַֽיְכוֹנְנ֑וּנִי הֲ֝בִינֵ֗נִי וְאֶלְמְדָ֥ה מִצְוֺתֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thy hands have made me and fashioned me: give me understanding, that I may learn thy commandments.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3027/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>yāḏ</em></a> (יָדֶ֣יךָ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3027/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H3027</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/74/" target="_blank">74</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">יְ֭רֵאֶיךָ</span> יִרְא֣וּנִי וְיִשְׂמָ֑חוּ כִּ֖י לִדְבָרְךָ֣ יִחָֽלְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">They that fear thee will be glad when they see me; because I have hoped in thy word.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3373/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>yārē'</em></a> (יְ֭רֵאֶיךָ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3373/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H3373</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/75/" target="_blank">75</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">יָדַ֣עְתִּי</span> יְ֭הוָה כִּי צֶ֣דֶק מִשְׁפָּטֶ֑יךָ וֶ֝אֱמוּנָ֗ה עִנִּיתָֽנִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I know, O LORD, that thy judgments are right, and that thou in faithfulness hast afflicted me.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3045/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>yāḏaʿ</em></a> (יָדַ֣עְתִּי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3045/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H3045</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/76/" target="_blank">76</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">יְהִי</span> נָ֣א חַסְדְּךָ֣ לְנַחֲמֵ֑נִי כְּאִמְרָתְךָ֥ לְעַבְדֶּֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Let, I pray thee, thy merciful kindness be for my comfort, according to thy word unto thy servant.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1961/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>hāyâ</em></a> (יְהִי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1961/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1961</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/77/" target="_blank">77</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">יְבֹא֣וּנִי</span> רַחֲמֶ֣יךָ וְאֶֽחְיֶ֑ה כִּי תֽ֝וֹרָתְךָ֗ שַֽׁעֲשֻׁעָֽי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Let thy tender mercies come unto me, that I may live: for thy law is my delight.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h935/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>bô'</em></a> (יְבֹא֣וּנִי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h935/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H935</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/78/" target="_blank">78</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">יֵבֹ֣שׁוּ</span> זֵ֭דִים כִּי שֶׁ֣קֶר עִוְּת֑וּנִי אֲ֝נִ֗י אָשִׂ֥יחַ בְּפִקּוּדֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Let the proud be ashamed; for they dealt perversely with me without a cause: but I will meditate in thy precepts.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h954/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>bûš</em></a> (יֵבֹ֣שׁוּ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h954/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H954</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/79/" target="_blank">79</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">יָשׁ֣וּבוּ</span> לִ֣י יְרֵאֶ֑יךָ וידעו עֵדֹתֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Let those that fear thee turn unto me, and those that have known thy testimonies.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7725/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>šûḇ</em></a> (יָשׁ֣וּבוּ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7725/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H7725</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/80/" target="_blank">80</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">יְהִֽי</span> לִבִּ֣י תָמִ֣ים בְּחֻקֶּ֑יךָ לְ֝מַ֗עַן לֹ֣א אֵבֽוֹשׁ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Let my heart be sound in thy statutes; that I be not ashamed.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1961/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>hāyâ</em></a> (יְהִֽי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1961/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1961</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</details>
</article>
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-11">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">כ</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Kaph <span class="lm-pron">k, k(ch)</span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">11 of 22 &middot; numeric value 20/500 &middot; Psalm 119:81&ndash;88</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">Eleventh letter, value 20; final form ך , value 500. The name means palm of the hand . Sounds k ; without the inner dot, kh .</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>an open palm</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>Prefixed כְּ — like, as , the particle of comparison. Suffixed ךָ — your . One of the six begadkephat letters.</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓂧</i><u>open palm</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">k</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤊</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡊</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">כ</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Κ</i><u></u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Kk</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:81&ndash;88 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/81/" target="_blank">81</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">כָּלְתָ֣ה</span> לִתְשׁוּעָתְךָ֣ נַפְשִׁ֑י לִדְבָרְךָ֥ יִחָֽלְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">My soul fainteth for thy salvation: but I hope in thy word.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3615/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>kâlâh</em></a> (כָּלְתָ֣ה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3615/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H3615</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/82/" target="_blank">82</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">כָּל֣וּ</span> עֵ֭ינַי לְאִמְרָתֶ֑ךָ לֵ֝אמֹ֗ר מָתַ֥י תְּֽנַחֲמֵֽנִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Mine eyes fail for thy word, saying, When wilt thou comfort me?</div>
