<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3615/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>kâlâh</em></a> (כָּל֣וּ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3615/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H3615</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/83/" target="_blank">83</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">כִּֽי</span> הָ֭יִיתִי כְּנֹ֣אד בְּקִיט֑וֹר חֻ֝קֶּ֗יךָ לֹ֣א שָׁכָֽחְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">For I am become like a bottle in the smoke; yet do I not forget thy statutes.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3588/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>kî</em></a> (כִּֽי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3588/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H3588</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/84/" target="_blank">84</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">כַּמָּ֥ה</span> יְמֵֽי עַבְדֶּ֑ךָ מָתַ֬י תַּעֲשֶׂ֖ה בְרֹדְפַ֣י מִשְׁפָּֽט</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">How many are the days of thy servant? when wilt thou execute judgment on them that persecute me?</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h4100/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>mâ</em></a> (כַּמָּ֥ה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h4100/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H4100</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/85/" target="_blank">85</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">כָּֽרוּ</span> לִ֣י זֵדִ֣ים שִׁיח֑וֹת אֲ֝שֶׁ֗ר לֹ֣א כְתוֹרָתֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">The proud have digged pits for me, which are not after thy law.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3738/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>kārâ</em></a> (כָּֽרוּ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3738/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H3738</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/86/" target="_blank">86</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">כָּל</span> מִצְוֺתֶ֥יךָ אֱמוּנָ֑ה שֶׁ֖קֶר רְדָפ֣וּנִי עָזְרֵֽנִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">All thy commandments are faithful: they persecute me wrongfully; help thou me.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3605/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>kôl</em></a> (כָּל) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3605/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H3605</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/87/" target="_blank">87</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">כִּ֭מְעַט</span> כִּלּ֣וּנִי בָאָ֑רֶץ וַ֝אֲנִ֗י לֹא עָזַ֥בְתִּי פִקֻּודֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">They had almost consumed me upon earth; but I forsook not thy precepts.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h4592/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>mᵊʿaṭ</em></a> (כִּ֭מְעַט) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h4592/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H4592</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/88/" target="_blank">88</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">כְּחַסְדְּךָ֥</span> חַיֵּ֑נִי וְ֝אֶשְׁמְרָ֗ה עֵד֥וּת פִּֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Quicken me after thy lovingkindness; so shall I keep the testimony of thy mouth.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2617/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ḥeseḏ</em></a> (כְּחַסְדְּךָ֥) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2617/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2617</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</details>
</article>
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-12">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">ל</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Lamed <span class="lm-pron">l</span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">12 of 22 &middot; numeric value 30 &middot; Psalm 119:89&ndash;96</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">Twelfth letter, value 30. The name is a shepherd&#x27;s staff or goad, and the root l-m-d gives both to learn and to teach . The only letter that rises above the line.</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>a shepherd&#x27;s staff or goad</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>Prefixed לְ — to, for, belonging to . Marks the indirect object, and forms the infinitive — to write .</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓋴</i><u>shepherd crook</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">l</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤋</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡋</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">ל</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Λ</i><u></u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Ll</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:89&ndash;96 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/89/" target="_blank">89</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">לְעוֹלָ֥ם</span> יְהוָ֑ה דְּ֝בָרְךָ֗ נִצָּ֥ב בַּשָּׁמָֽיִם</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">For ever, O LORD, thy word is settled in heaven.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5769/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ʿôlām</em></a> (לְעוֹלָ֥ם) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5769/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5769</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/90/" target="_blank">90</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">לְדֹ֣ר</span> וָ֭דֹר אֱמֽוּנָתֶ֑ךָ כּוֹנַ֥נְתָּ אֶ֝֗רֶץ וַֽתַּעֲמֹֽד</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thy faithfulness is unto all generations: thou hast established the earth, and it abideth.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1755/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>dôr</em></a> (לְדֹ֣ר) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h1755/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H1755</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/91/" target="_blank">91</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">לְֽ֭מִשְׁפָּטֶיךָ</span> עָמְד֣וּ הַיּ֑וֹם כִּ֖י הַכֹּ֣ל עֲבָדֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">They continue this day according to thine ordinances: for all are thy servants.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h4941/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>mišpāṭ</em></a> (לְֽ֭מִשְׁפָּטֶיךָ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h4941/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H4941</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/92/" target="_blank">92</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">לוּלֵ֣י</span> ת֭וֹרָתְךָ שַׁעֲשֻׁעָ֑י אָ֝֗ז אָבַ֥דְתִּי בְעָנְיִֽי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Unless thy law had been my delights, I should then have perished in mine affliction.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3884/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>lûlē'</em></a> (לוּלֵ֣י) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3884/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H3884</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/93/" target="_blank">93</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">לְ֭עוֹלָם</span> לֹא אֶשְׁכַּ֣ח פִּקּוּדֶ֑יךָ כִּ֥י בָ֝֗ם חִיִּיתָֽנִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I will never forget thy precepts: for with them thou hast quickened me.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5769/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ʿôlām</em></a> (לְ֭עוֹלָם) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5769/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5769</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/94/" target="_blank">94</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">לְֽךָ</span> אֲ֭נִי הוֹשִׁיעֵ֑נִי כִּ֖י פִקּוּדֶ֣יךָ דָרָֽשְׁתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I am thine, save me; for I have sought thy precepts.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> (לְֽךָ) &middot; <span class="lm-nolex">prefix particle — no separate lexicon entry</span></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/95/" target="_blank">95</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">לִ֤י</span> קִוּ֣וּ רְשָׁעִ֣ים לְאַבְּדֵ֑נִי עֵ֝דֹתֶ֗יךָ אֶתְבּוֹנָֽן</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">The wicked have waited for me to destroy me: but I will consider thy testimonies.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> (לִ֤י) &middot; <span class="lm-nolex">prefix particle — no separate lexicon entry</span></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/96/" target="_blank">96</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">לְֽכָל</span> תִּ֭כְלָה רָאִ֣יתִי קֵ֑ץ רְחָבָ֖ה מִצְוָתְךָ֣ מְאֹֽד</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I have seen an end of all perfection: but thy commandment is exceeding broad.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3605/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>kôl</em></a> (לְֽכָל) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3605/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H3605</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</details>
</article>
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-13">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">מ</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Mem <span class="lm-pron">m</span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">13 of 22 &middot; numeric value 40/600 &middot; Psalm 119:97&ndash;104</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">Thirteenth letter, value 40; final form ם , value 600. The name means water , and the ancient form is a wave. Sounds m .</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>water — a wave</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>Prefixed מִ — from, out of . Also turns verbs into nouns : a root meaning to be holy gives miqdash , sanctuary — nouns of place and instrument. Suffixed ִים — the masculine plural.</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓈖</i><u>water</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">m</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤌</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡌</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">מ</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Μ</i><u></u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Mm</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:97&ndash;104 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/97/" target="_blank">97</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">מָֽה</span> אָהַ֥בְתִּי תוֹרָתֶ֑ךָ כָּל הַ֝יּ֗וֹם הִ֣יא שִׂיחָתִֽי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">O how love I thy law! it is my meditation all the day.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h4100/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>mâ</em></a> (מָֽה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h4100/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H4100</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/98/" target="_blank">98</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">מֵ֭אֹ֣יְבַי</span> תְּחַכְּמֵ֣נִי מִצְוֺתֶ֑ךָ כִּ֖י לְעוֹלָ֣ם הִיא לִֽי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thou through thy commandments hast made me wiser than mine enemies: for they are ever with me.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h341/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>'ōyēḇ</em></a> (מֵ֭אֹ֣יְבַי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h341/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H341</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/99/" target="_blank">99</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">מִכָּל</span> מְלַמְּדַ֥י הִשְׂכַּ֑לְתִּי כִּ֥י עֵ֝דְוֺתֶ֗יךָ שִׂ֣יחָה לִֽֿי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I have more understanding than all my teachers: for thy testimonies are my meditation.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3605/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>kôl</em></a> (מִכָּל) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3605/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H3605</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/100/" target="_blank">100</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">מִזְּקֵנִ֥ים</span> אֶתְבּוֹנָ֑ן כִּ֖י פִקּוּדֶ֣יךָ נָצָֽרְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I understand more than the ancients, because I keep thy precepts.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2205/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>zâqên</em></a> (מִזְּקֵנִ֥ים) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h2205/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H2205</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/101/" target="_blank">101</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">מִכָּל</span> אֹ֣רַח רָ֭ע כָּלִ֣אתִי רַגְלָ֑י לְ֝מַ֗עַן אֶשְׁמֹ֥ר דְּבָרֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I have refrained my feet from every evil way, that I might keep thy word.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3605/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>kôl</em></a> (מִכָּל) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h3605/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H3605</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/102/" target="_blank">102</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">מִמִּשְׁפָּטֶ֥יךָ</span> לֹא סָ֑רְתִּי כִּֽי אַ֝תָּ֗ה הוֹרֵתָֽנִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I have not departed from thy judgments: for thou hast taught me.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h4941/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>mišpāṭ</em></a> (מִמִּשְׁפָּטֶ֥יךָ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h4941/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H4941</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/103/" target="_blank">103</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">מַה</span> נִּמְלְצ֣וּ לְ֭חִכִּי אִמְרָתֶ֗ךָ מִדְּבַ֥שׁ לְפִֽי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">How sweet are thy words unto my taste! yea, sweeter than honey to my mouth!</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h4100/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>mâ</em></a> (מַה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h4100/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H4100</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/104/" target="_blank">104</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">מִפִּקּוּדֶ֥יךָ</span> אֶתְבּוֹנָ֑ן עַל כֵּ֝֗ן שָׂנֵ֤אתִי כָּל אֹ֬רַח שָֽׁקֶר</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Through thy precepts I get understanding: therefore I hate every false way.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6490/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>piqqûḏîm</em></a> (מִפִּקּוּדֶ֥יךָ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6490/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6490</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</details>
</article>
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-14">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">נ</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Nun <span class="lm-pron">n</span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">14 of 22 &middot; numeric value 50/700 &middot; Psalm 119:105&ndash;112</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">Fourteenth letter, value 50; final form ן , value 700. The name means fish in Aramaic; the ancient form is a sprout or serpent. Sounds n .</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>a sprout or seed; a serpent or fish</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>Prefixed, marks first person plural in the imperfect — we will… . Also marks the niphal , the passive and reflexive verb pattern.</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓆓</i><u>snake/fish</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">n</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤍</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡍</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">נ</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Ν</i><u></u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Nn</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:105&ndash;112 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/105/" target="_blank">105</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">נֵר</span> לְרַגְלִ֥י דְבָרֶ֑ךָ וְ֝א֗וֹר לִנְתִיבָתִֽי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thy word is a lamp unto my feet, and a light unto my path.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5216/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>nîr</em></a> (נֵר) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5216/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5216</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/106/" target="_blank">106</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">נִשְׁבַּ֥עְתִּי</span> וָאֲקַיֵּ֑מָה לִ֝שְׁמֹ֗ר מִשְׁפְּטֵ֥י צִדְקֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I have sworn, and I will perform it, that I will keep thy righteous judgments.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7650/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>šāḇaʿ</em></a> (נִשְׁבַּ֥עְתִּי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7650/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H7650</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/107/" target="_blank">107</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">נַעֲנֵ֥יתִי</span> עַד מְאֹ֑ד יְ֝הוָ֗ה חַיֵּ֥נִי כִדְבָרֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I am afflicted very much: quicken me, O LORD, according unto thy word.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6031/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ʿānâ</em></a> (נַעֲנֵ֥יתִי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6031/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6031</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/108/" target="_blank">108</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">נִדְב֣וֹת</span> פִּ֭י רְצֵה נָ֣א יְהוָ֑ה וּֽמִשְׁפָּטֶ֥יךָ לַמְּדֵֽנִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Accept, I beseech thee, the freewill offerings of my mouth, O LORD, and teach me thy judgments.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5071/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>nᵊḏāḇâ</em></a> (נִדְב֣וֹת) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5071/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5071</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/109/" target="_blank">109</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">נַפְשִׁ֣י</span> בְכַפִּ֣י תָמִ֑יד וְ֝תֽוֹרָתְךָ֗ לֹ֣א שָׁכָֽחְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">My soul is continually in my hand: yet do I not forget thy law.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5315/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>nep̄eš</em></a> (נַפְשִׁ֣י) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5315/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5315</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/110/" target="_blank">110</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">נָתְנ֬וּ</span> רְשָׁעִ֣ים פַּ֣ח לִ֑י וּ֝מִפִּקּוּדֶ֗יךָ לֹ֣א תָעִֽיתִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">The wicked have laid a snare for me: yet I erred not from thy precepts.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5414/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>nāṯan</em></a> (נָתְנ֬וּ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5414/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5414</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/111/" target="_blank">111</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">נָחַ֣לְתִּי</span> עֵדְוֺתֶ֣יךָ לְעוֹלָ֑ם כִּֽי שְׂשׂ֖וֹן לִבִּ֣י הֵֽמָּה</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thy testimonies have I taken as an heritage for ever: for they are the rejoicing of my heart.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5157/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>nāḥal</em></a> (נָחַ֣לְתִּי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5157/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5157</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/112/" target="_blank">112</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">נָטִ֣יתִי</span> לִ֭בִּי לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת חֻקֶּ֗יךָ לְעוֹלָ֥ם עֵֽקֶב</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I have inclined mine heart to perform thy statutes alway, even unto the end.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5186/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>nāṭâ</em></a> (נָטִ֣יתִי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5186/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5186</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</details>
</article>
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-15">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">ס</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Samech <span class="lm-pron">s</span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">15 of 22 &middot; numeric value 60 &middot; Psalm 119:113&ndash;120</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">Fifteenth letter, value 60. The name is linked to support ; the ancient form is a pillar. Sounds s .</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>a pillar or support</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>No common prefix or grammatical function — it appears only as a root letter.</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓊽</i><u>djed pillar/support</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">s</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤎</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡎</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">ס</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Ξ</i><u></u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Ss, Cc</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:113&ndash;120 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/113/" target="_blank">113</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">סֵעֲפִ֥ים</span> שָׂנֵ֑אתִי וְֽתוֹרָתְךָ֥ אָהָֽבְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I hate vain thoughts: but thy law do I love.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5588/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>sēʿēp̄</em></a> (סֵעֲפִ֥ים) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5588/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5588</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/114/" target="_blank">114</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">סִתְרִ֣י</span> וּמָגִנִּ֣י אָ֑תָּה לִדְבָרְךָ֥ יִחָֽלְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thou art my hiding place and my shield: I hope in thy word.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5643/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>sēṯer</em></a> (סִתְרִ֣י) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5643/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5643</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/115/" target="_blank">115</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">סֽוּרוּ</span> מִמֶּ֥נִּי מְרֵעִ֑ים וְ֝אֶצְּרָ֗ה מִצְוֺ֥ת אֱלֹהָֽי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Depart from me, ye evildoers: for I will keep the commandments of my God.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5493/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>sûr</em></a> (סֽוּרוּ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5493/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5493</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/116/" target="_blank">116</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">סָמְכֵ֣נִי</span> כְאִמְרָתְךָ֣ וְאֶֽחְיֶ֑ה וְאַל תְּ֝בִישֵׁ֗נִי מִשִּׂבְרִֽי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Uphold me according unto thy word, that I may live: and let me not be ashamed of my hope.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5564/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>sāmaḵ</em></a> (סָמְכֵ֣נִי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5564/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5564</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/117/" target="_blank">117</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">סְעָדֵ֥נִי</span> וְאִוָּשֵׁ֑עָה וְאֶשְׁעָ֖ה בְחֻקֶּ֣יךָ תָמִֽיד</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Hold thou me up, and I shall be safe: and I will have respect unto thy statutes continually.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5582/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>sāʿaḏ</em></a> (סְעָדֵ֥נִי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5582/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5582</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/118/" target="_blank">118</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">סָ֭לִיתָ</span> כָּל שׁוֹגִ֣ים מֵחֻקֶּ֑יךָ כִּי שֶׁ֝֗קֶר תַּרְמִיתָֽם</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thou hast trodden down all them that err from thy statutes: for their deceit is falsehood.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5541/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>sālâ</em></a> (סָ֭לִיתָ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5541/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5541</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/119/" target="_blank">119</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">סִגִ֗ים</span> הִשְׁבַּ֥תָּ כָל רִשְׁעֵי אָ֑רֶץ לָ֝כֵ֗ן אָהַ֥בְתִּי עֵדֹתֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thou puttest away all the wicked of the earth like dross: therefore I love thy testimonies.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5509/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>sîḡ</em></a> (סִגִ֗ים) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5509/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5509</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/120/" target="_blank">120</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">סָמַ֣ר</span> מִפַּחְדְּךָ֣ בְשָׂרִ֑י וּֽמִמִּשְׁפָּטֶ֥יךָ יָרֵֽאתִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">My flesh trembleth for fear of thee; and I am afraid of thy judgments.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5568/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>sāmar</em></a> (סָמַ֣ר) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5568/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5568</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</details>
</article>
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-16">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">ע</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Ayin <span class="lm-pron">ʿ (ayin)</span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">16 of 22 &middot; numeric value 70 &middot; Psalm 119:121&ndash;128</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">Sixteenth letter, value 70. The name means eye . A voiced pharyngeal — silent to most English speakers, though it shapes the vowel around it.</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>an eye; a well or spring</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>No common prefix or grammatical function — it appears only as a root letter.</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓁹</i><u>eye</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">[</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤏</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡏</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">ע</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Ο</i><u></u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Oo</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:121&ndash;128 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/121/" target="_blank">121</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">עָ֭שִׂיתִי</span> מִשְׁפָּ֣ט וָצֶ֑דֶק בַּל תַּ֝נִּיחֵ֗נִי לְעֹֽשְׁקָֽי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I have done judgment and justice: leave me not to mine oppressors.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6213/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ʿāśâ</em></a> (עָ֭שִׂיתִי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6213/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6213</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/122/" target="_blank">122</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">עֲרֹ֣ב</span> עַבְדְּךָ֣ לְט֑וֹב אַֽל יַעַשְׁקֻ֥נִי זֵדִֽים</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Be surety for thy servant for good: let not the proud oppress me.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6148/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ʿāraḇ</em></a> (עֲרֹ֣ב) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6148/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6148</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/123/" target="_blank">123</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">עֵ֭ינַי</span> כָּל֣וּ לִֽישׁוּעָתֶ֑ךָ וּלְאִמְרַ֥ת צִדְקֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Mine eyes fail for thy salvation, and for the word of thy righteousness.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5869/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ʿayin</em></a> (עֵ֭ינַי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5869/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5869</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/124/" target="_blank">124</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">עֲשֵׂ֖ה</span> עִם עַבְדְּךָ֥ כְחַסְדֶּ֗ךָ וְחֻקֶּ֥יךָ לַמְּדֵֽנִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Deal with thy servant according unto thy mercy, and teach me thy statutes.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6213/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ʿāśâ</em></a> (עֲשֵׂ֖ה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6213/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6213</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/125/" target="_blank">125</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">עַבְדְּךָ</span> אָ֥נִי הֲבִינֵ֑נִי וְ֝אֵדְעָ֗ה עֵדֹתֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I am thy servant; give me understanding, that I may know thy testimonies.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5650/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ʿeḇeḏ</em></a> (עַבְדְּךָ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5650/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5650</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/126/" target="_blank">126</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">עֵ֭ת</span> לַעֲשׂ֣וֹת לַיהוָ֑ה הֵ֝פֵ֗רוּ תּוֹרָתֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">It is time for thee, LORD, to work: for they have made void thy law.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6256/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ʿēṯ</em></a> (עֵ֭ת) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6256/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6256</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/127/" target="_blank">127</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">עַל</span> כֵּ֭ן אָהַ֣בְתִּי מִצְוֺתֶ֑יךָ מִזָּהָ֥ב וּמִפָּֽז</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Therefore I love thy commandments above gold; yea, above fine gold.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5921/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ʿal</em></a> (עַל) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5921/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5921</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/128/" target="_blank">128</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">עַל</span> כֵּ֤ן כָּל פִּקּ֣וּדֵי כֹ֣ל יִשָּׁ֑רְתִּי כָּל אֹ֖רַח שֶׁ֣קֶר שָׂנֵֽאתִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Therefore I esteem all thy precepts concerning all things to be right; and I hate every false way.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5921/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ʿal</em></a> (עַל) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h5921/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H5921</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</details>
</article>
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-17">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">פ</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Pey, Fe <span class="lm-pron">p, p̄(f)</span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">17 of 22 &middot; numeric value 80/800 &middot; Psalm 119:129&ndash;136</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">Seventeenth letter, value 80; final form ף , value 800. The name means mouth . Sounds p ; without the inner dot, f .</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>a mouth; a corner</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>One of the six begadkephat letters. No common prefix use.</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓂋</i><u>mouth</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">p</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤐</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡐</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">פ</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Π</i><u></u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Pp, Ff, Ph</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:129&ndash;136 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/129/" target="_blank">129</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">פְּלָא֥וֹת</span> עֵדְוֺתֶ֑יךָ עַל כֵּ֝֗ן נְצָרָ֥תַם נַפְשִֽׁי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thy testimonies are wonderful: therefore doth my soul keep them.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6382/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>pele'</em></a> (פְּלָא֥וֹת) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6382/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6382</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/130/" target="_blank">130</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">פֵּ֖תַח</span> דְּבָרֶ֥יךָ יָאִ֗יר מֵבִ֥ין פְּתָיִֽים</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">The entrance of thy words giveth light; it giveth understanding unto the simple.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6608/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>pēṯaḥ</em></a> (פֵּ֖תַח) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6608/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6608</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/131/" target="_blank">131</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">פִּֽי</span> פָ֭עַרְתִּי וָאֶשְׁאָ֑פָה כִּ֖י לְמִצְוֺתֶ֣יךָ יָאָֽבְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I opened my mouth, and panted: for I longed for thy commandments.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6310/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>pê</em></a> (פִּֽי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6310/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6310</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/132/" target="_blank">132</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">פְּנֵה</span> אֵלַ֥י וְחָנֵּ֑נִי כְּ֝מִשְׁפָּ֗ט לְאֹהֲבֵ֥י שְׁמֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Look thou upon me, and be merciful unto me, as thou usest to do unto those that love thy name.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6437/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>pānâ</em></a> (פְּנֵה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6437/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6437</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/133/" target="_blank">133</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">פְּ֭עָמַי</span> הָכֵ֣ן בְּאִמְרָתֶ֑ךָ וְֽאַל תַּשְׁלֶט בִּ֥י כָל אָֽוֶן</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Order my steps in thy word: and let not any iniquity have dominion over me.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6471/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>paʿam</em></a> (פְּ֭עָמַי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6471/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6471</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/134/" target="_blank">134</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">פְּ֭דֵנִי</span> מֵעֹ֣שֶׁק אָדָ֑ם וְ֝אֶשְׁמְרָ֗ה פִּקּוּדֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Deliver me from the oppression of man: so will I keep thy precepts.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6299/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>pāḏâ</em></a> (פְּ֭דֵנִי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6299/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6299</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/135/" target="_blank">135</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">פָּ֭נֶיךָ</span> הָאֵ֣ר בְּעַבְדֶּ֑ךָ וְ֝לַמְּדֵ֗נִי אֶת חֻקֶּֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Make thy face to shine upon thy servant; and teach me thy statutes.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6440/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>pānîm</em></a> (פָּ֭נֶיךָ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6440/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6440</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/136/" target="_blank">136</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">פַּלְגֵי</span> מַ֭יִם יָרְד֣וּ עֵינָ֑י עַ֝֗ל לֹא שָׁמְר֥וּ תוֹרָתֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Rivers of waters run down mine eyes, because they keep not thy law.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6388/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>peleḡ</em></a> (פַּלְגֵי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6388/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6388</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</details>
</article>
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-18">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">צ</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Tzaddi ṣ <span class="lm-pron"></span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">18 of 22 &middot; numeric value 90/900 &middot; Psalm 119:137&ndash;144</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">Eighteenth letter, value 90; final form ץ , value 900. The ancient form is a harpoon or plant stalk. An emphatic s , pronounced ts .</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>a harpoon, hook or plant stalk</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>No common prefix or grammatical function — it appears only as a root letter.</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓇯</i><u>papyrus/plant</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">c</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤑</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡑</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">צ</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Ϻ</i><u>San</u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Tz</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:137&ndash;144 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/137/" target="_blank">137</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">צַדִּ֣יק</span> אַתָּ֣ה יְהוָ֑ה וְ֝יָשָׁ֗ר מִשְׁפָּטֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Righteous art thou, O LORD, and upright are thy judgments.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6662/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ṣadîq</em></a> (צַדִּ֣יק) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6662/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6662</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/138/" target="_blank">138</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">צִ֭וִּיתָ</span> צֶ֣דֶק עֵדֹתֶ֑יךָ וֶֽאֱמוּנָ֥ה מְאֹֽד</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thy testimonies that thou hast commanded are righteous and very faithful.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6680/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ṣāvâ</em></a> (צִ֭וִּיתָ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6680/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6680</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/139/" target="_blank">139</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">צִמְּתַ֥תְנִי</span> קִנְאָתִ֑י כִּֽי שָׁכְח֖וּ דְבָרֶ֣יךָ צָרָֽי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">My zeal hath consumed me, because mine enemies have forgotten thy words.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6789/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ṣāmaṯ</em></a> (צִמְּתַ֥תְנִי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6789/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6789</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/140/" target="_blank">140</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">צְרוּפָ֖ה</span> אִמְרָתְךָ֥ מְאֹ֗ד וְֽעַבְדְּךָ֥ אֲהֵבָֽהּ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thy word is very pure: therefore thy servant loveth it.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6884/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ṣārap̄</em></a> (צְרוּפָ֖ה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6884/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6884</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/141/" target="_blank">141</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">צָעִ֣יר</span> אָנֹכִ֣י וְנִבְזֶ֑ה פִּ֝קֻּדֶ֗יךָ לֹ֣א שָׁכָֽחְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I am small and despised: yet do not I forget thy precepts.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6810/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ṣāʿîr</em></a> (צָעִ֣יר) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6810/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6810</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/142/" target="_blank">142</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">צִדְקָתְךָ֣</span> צֶ֣דֶק לְעוֹלָ֑ם וְֽתוֹרָתְךָ֥ אֱמֶֽת</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thy righteousness is an everlasting righteousness, and thy law is the truth.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6666/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ṣᵊḏāqâ</em></a> (צִדְקָתְךָ֣) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6666/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6666</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/143/" target="_blank">143</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">צַר</span> וּמָצ֥וֹק מְצָא֑וּנִי מִ֝צְוֺתֶ֗יךָ שַׁעֲשֻׁעָֽי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Trouble and anguish have taken hold on me: yet thy commandments are my delights.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6862/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ṣar</em></a> (צַר) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6862/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6862</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/144/" target="_blank">144</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">צֶ֖דֶק</span> עֵדְוֺתֶ֥יךָ לְעוֹלָ֗ם הֲבִינֵ֥נִי וְאֶחְיֶֽה</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">The righteousness of thy testimonies is everlasting: give me understanding, and I shall live.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6664/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>ṣeḏeq</em></a> (צֶ֖דֶק) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6664/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6664</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</details>
</article>
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-19">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">ק</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Quph <span class="lm-pron">q</span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">19 of 22 &middot; numeric value 100 &middot; Psalm 119:145&ndash;152</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">Nineteenth letter, value 100. The name is linked to the eye of a needle, or to the back of the head. A k made further back in the throat than kaph.</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>the eye of a needle; a cord or knot — this one is debated; scholars also propose the back of the head</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>No common prefix or grammatical function — it appears only as a root letter.</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓏤</i><u>cord/knot</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">q</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤒</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡒</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">ק</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Ϙ</i><u>Qoppa</u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Qq</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:145&ndash;152 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/145/" target="_blank">145</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">קָרָ֣אתִי</span> בְכָל לֵ֭ב עֲנֵ֥נִי יְהוָ֗ה חֻקֶּ֥יךָ אֶצֹּֽרָה</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I cried with my whole heart; hear me, O LORD: I will keep thy statutes.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7121/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>qārā'</em></a> (קָרָ֣אתִי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7121/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H7121</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/146/" target="_blank">146</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">קְרָאתִ֥יךָ</span> הוֹשִׁיעֵ֑נִי וְ֝אֶשְׁמְרָ֗ה עֵדֹתֶֽיךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I cried unto thee; save me, and I shall keep thy testimonies.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7121/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>qārā'</em></a> (קְרָאתִ֥יךָ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7121/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H7121</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/147/" target="_blank">147</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">קִדַּ֣מְתִּי</span> בַ֭נֶּשֶׁף וָאֲשַׁוֵּ֑עָה לדבריך יִחָֽלְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I prevented the dawning of the morning, and cried: I hoped in thy word.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6923/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>qāḏam</em></a> (קִדַּ֣מְתִּי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6923/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6923</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/148/" target="_blank">148</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">קִדְּמ֣וּ</span> עֵ֭ינַי אַשְׁמֻר֑וֹת לָ֝שִׂ֗יחַ בְּאִמְרָתֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Mine eyes prevent the night watches, that I might meditate in thy word.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6923/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>qāḏam</em></a> (קִדְּמ֣וּ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6923/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6923</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/149/" target="_blank">149</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">ק֭וֹלִי</span> שִׁמְעָ֣ה כְחַסְדֶּ֑ךָ יְ֝הוָ֗ה כְּֽמִשְׁפָּטֶ֥ךָ חַיֵּֽנִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Hear my voice according unto thy lovingkindness: O LORD, quicken me according to thy judgment.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6963/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>qôl</em></a> (ק֭וֹלִי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6963/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6963</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/150/" target="_blank">150</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">קָ֭רְבוּ</span> רֹדְפֵ֣י זִמָּ֑ה מִתּוֹרָתְךָ֥ רָחָֽקוּ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">They draw nigh that follow after mischief: they are far from thy law.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7126/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>qāraḇ</em></a> (קָ֭רְבוּ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7126/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H7126</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/151/" target="_blank">151</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">קָר֣וֹב</span> אַתָּ֣ה יְהוָ֑ה וְֽכָל מִצְוֺתֶ֥יךָ אֱמֶֽת</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thou art near, O LORD; and all thy commandments are truth.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7138/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>qārôḇ</em></a> (קָר֣וֹב) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7138/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H7138</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/152/" target="_blank">152</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">קֶ֣דֶם</span> יָ֭דַעְתִּי מֵעֵדֹתֶ֑יךָ כִּ֖י לְעוֹלָ֣ם יְסַדְתָּֽם</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Concerning thy testimonies, I have known of old that thou hast founded them for ever.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6924/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>qeḏem</em></a> (קֶ֣דֶם) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h6924/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H6924</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</details>
</article>
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-20">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">ר</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Resh <span class="lm-pron">r</span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">20 of 22 &middot; numeric value 200 &middot; Psalm 119:153&ndash;160</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">Twentieth letter, value 200. The name means head , and the related root gives beginning and chief . Sounds r .</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>a head in profile</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>No common prefix or grammatical function — it appears only as a root letter.</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓁶</i><u>head profile</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">r</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤓</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡓</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">ר</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Ρ</i><u></u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Rr</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:153&ndash;160 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/153/" target="_blank">153</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">רְאֵֽה</span> עָנְיִ֥י וְחַלְּצֵ֑נִי כִּי תֽ֝וֹרָתְךָ֗ לֹ֣א שָׁכָֽחְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Consider mine affliction, and deliver me: for I do not forget thy law.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7200/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>rā'â</em></a> (רְאֵֽה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7200/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H7200</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/154/" target="_blank">154</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">רִיבָ֣ה</span> רִ֭יבִי וּגְאָלֵ֑נִי לְאִמְרָתְךָ֥ חַיֵּֽנִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Plead my cause, and deliver me: quicken me according to thy word.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7378/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>rîḇ</em></a> (רִיבָ֣ה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7378/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H7378</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/155/" target="_blank">155</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">רָח֣וֹק</span> מֵרְשָׁעִ֣ים יְשׁוּעָ֑ה כִּֽי חֻ֝קֶּיךָ לֹ֣א דָרָֽשׁוּ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Salvation is far from the wicked: for they seek not thy statutes.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7350/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>rāḥôq</em></a> (רָח֣וֹק) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7350/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H7350</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/156/" target="_blank">156</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">רַחֲמֶ֖יךָ</span> רַבִּ֥ים יְהוָ֑ה כְּֽמִשְׁפָּטֶ֥יךָ חַיֵּֽנִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Great are thy tender mercies, O LORD: quicken me according to thy judgments.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7356/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>raḥam</em></a> (רַחֲמֶ֖יךָ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7356/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H7356</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/157/" target="_blank">157</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">רַ֭בִּים</span> רֹדְפַ֣י וְצָרָ֑י מֵ֝עֵדְוֺתֶ֗יךָ לֹ֣א נָטִֽיתִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Many are my persecutors and mine enemies; yet do I not decline from thy testimonies.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7227/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>raḇ</em></a> (רַ֭בִּים) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7227/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H7227</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/158/" target="_blank">158</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">רָאִ֣יתִי</span> בֹ֭גְדִים וָֽאֶתְקוֹטָ֑טָה אֲשֶׁ֥ר אִ֝מְרָתְךָ֗ לֹ֣א שָׁמָֽרוּ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I beheld the transgressors, and was grieved; because they kept not thy word.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7200/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>rā'â</em></a> (רָאִ֣יתִי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7200/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H7200</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/159/" target="_blank">159</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">רְ֭אֵה</span> כִּי פִקּוּדֶ֣יךָ אָהָ֑בְתִּי יְ֝הוָ֗ה כְּֽחַסְדְּךָ֥ חַיֵּֽנִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Consider how I love thy precepts: quicken me, O LORD, according to thy lovingkindness.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7200/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>rā'â</em></a> (רְ֭אֵה) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7200/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H7200</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/160/" target="_blank">160</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">רֹאשׁ</span> דְּבָרְךָ֥ אֱמֶ֑ת וּ֝לְעוֹלָ֗ם כָּל מִשְׁפַּ֥ט צִדְקֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Thy word is true from the beginning: and every one of thy righteous judgments endureth for ever.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7218/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>rō'š</em></a> (רֹאשׁ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7218/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H7218</a></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</details>
</article>
<article class="lm-card" id="letter-21">
<header class="lm-head">
<div class="lm-glyph">ש</div>
<div class="lm-id">
<h3>Sin, Shin ś(s), š(sh) <span class="lm-pron"></span></h3>
<div class="lm-meta">21 of 22 &middot; numeric value 300 &middot; Psalm 119:161&ndash;168</div>
</div>
</header>
<p class="lm-def">Twenty-first letter, value 300. The name means tooth . With the dot at upper right it is sh ; at upper left, s — the same letter, two sounds.</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>teeth; a composite bow</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>Prefixed שֶׁ — that, which , the relative particle. Rare in early Biblical Hebrew, common in Ecclesiastes and Song of Songs, and standard in later Hebrew.</div>
<div class="lm-chain"><span><i class="lm-anc">𓌙</i><u>composite bow/teeth</u><em>Egyptian</em></span><span><i class="lm-ps">v</i><u></u><em>Proto-Sinaitic</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐤔</i><u></u><em>Phoenician</em></span><span><i class="lm-anc">𐡔</i><u></u><em>Aramaic</em></span><span><i class="lm-heb-g">ש</i><u></u><em>Hebrew</em></span><span><i class="">Σ</i><u></u><em>Greek</em></span><span><i class="">Ss, Sh</i><u></u><em>Latin</em></span></div>
<details class="lm-drop">
<summary>Read Psalm 119:161&ndash;168 &mdash; all eight verses</summary>
<div class="lm-verses">
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/161/" target="_blank">161</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">שָׂ֭רִים</span> רְדָפ֣וּנִי חִנָּ֑ם ומדבריך פָּחַ֥ד לִבִּֽי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Princes have persecuted me without a cause: but my heart standeth in awe of thy word.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h8269/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>śar</em></a> (שָׂ֭רִים) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h8269/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H8269</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/162/" target="_blank">162</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">שָׂ֣שׂ</span> אָ֭נֹכִֽי עַל אִמְרָתֶ֑ךָ כְּ֝מוֹצֵ֗א שָׁלָ֥ל רָֽב</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I rejoice at thy word, as one that findeth great spoil.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7797/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>śûś</em></a> (שָׂ֣שׂ) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7797/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H7797</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/163/" target="_blank">163</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">שֶׁ֣קֶר</span> שָׂ֭נֵאתִי וַאֲתַעֵ֑בָה תּוֹרָתְךָ֥ אָהָֽבְתִּי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">I hate and abhor lying: but thy law do I love.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h8267/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>šeqer</em></a> (שֶׁ֣קֶר) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h8267/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H8267</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/164/" target="_blank">164</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">שֶׁ֣בַע</span> בַּ֭יּוֹם הִלַּלְתִּ֑יךָ עַ֝֗ל מִשְׁפְּטֵ֥י צִדְקֶֽךָ</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Seven times a day do I praise thee because of thy righteous judgments.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7651/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>šeḇaʿ</em></a> (שֶׁ֣בַע) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7651/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H7651</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/165/" target="_blank">165</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">שָׁל֣וֹם</span> רָ֭ב לְאֹהֲבֵ֣י תוֹרָתֶ֑ךָ וְאֵֽין לָ֥מוֹ מִכְשֽׁוֹל</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">Great peace have they which love thy law: and nothing shall offend them.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7965/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>šālôm</em></a> (שָׁל֣וֹם) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7965/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H7965</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/166/" target="_blank">166</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">שִׂבַּ֣רְתִּי</span> לִֽישׁוּעָתְךָ֣ יְהוָ֑ה וּֽמִצְוֺתֶ֥יךָ עָשִֽׂיתִי</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">LORD, I have hoped for thy salvation, and done thy commandments.</div>
<div class="lm-word"><span class="lm-wlab">opens with</span> <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7663/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank"><em>śāḇar</em></a> (שִׂבַּ֣רְתִּי) &middot; <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/lexicon/h7663/kjv/wlc/0-1/" target="_blank">H7663</a></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="lm-v">
<div class="lm-vnum"><a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/167/" target="_blank">167</a></div>
<div class="lm-vbody">
<div class="lm-heb" dir="rtl" lang="he"><span class="lm-acro">שָֽׁמְרָ֣ה</span> נַ֭פְשִׁי עֵדֹתֶ֑יךָ וָאֹהֲבֵ֥ם מְאֹֽד</div>
<div class="lm-kjv">My soul hath kept thy testimonies; and I love them exceedingly.</div>

<h3>Tav <span class="lm-pron">t, ṯ</span></h3>

<p class="lm-def">Twenty-second and final letter, value 400. The name means mark or sign , and the ancient form is a cross. Sounds t .</p>
<div class="lm-sign"><span class="lm-glab">The sign shows</span>a cross or mark — a signature on a contract</div>
<div class="lm-gram"><span class="lm-glab">How it is used</span>Prefixed, marks second person in the imperfect — you will… — and third person feminine. Suffixed, the feminine ending, and forms abstract nouns. One of the six begadkephat letters.</div>

---

## The Whole Alphabet, Read This Way — and It Is Old

The *gimel* example above is not a curiosity someone noticed once. It is one link in a chain that runs the **entire alphabet**, and it sits in that same page of the Talmud.

In <a href="https://www.sefaria.org/Shabbat.104a" target="_blank">Shabbat 104a</a>, schoolchildren pair the letters off and read a lesson from each pair, straight through the alef-bet. The Sages record it because they found it remarkable — *"things the likes of which were not said even in the days of Joshua bin Nun."*

| the letters paired | read as | the lesson |
|---|---|---|
| **alef · bet** | *elaf bina* | learn the wisdom of the Torah |
| **gimel · dalet** | *gemol dalim* | give to the poor |
| **hey · vav** | — | the principal name of the Holy One |
| **zayin · chet · tet · yod · kaf · lamed** | *zan · chan · meitiv · yerusha · keter* | God feeds you, shows you favour, does good to you, gives you an inheritance, and ties a crown for you in the World to Come |
| **samech · ayin** | *semokh aniyyim* | support the poor before they fall further |
| *the same pair, another version* | *simanim aseh* | **make signs to remember the Torah and acquire it** |

It reads the **shapes** as well as the names. Why does the leg of the *gimel* reach toward the *dalet*? Because the giver pursues the poor. Why does the *dalet* face away? So that charity is given discreetly and the person receiving it is not embarrassed. The open and closed *mem* stand for a teaching that is plain and a teaching that is hidden; the bent and straight *nun*, for a faithful person who is modest now and will be known later.

**Why this belongs in a reader's map.** It shows that reading meaning in the letters is not a modern habit or a one-off. It is **systematic** — it runs the whole alphabet — and it is **old**, recorded in a mainstream legal compilation roughly seven hundred years before the medieval period it is sometimes credited to.

**A word about method, because this is easy to misuse.** This article does identify the letters. Their names are ordinary Hebrew words — *aleph* an ox, *bet* a house, *mem* water, *ayin* an eye — and the ancient signs are pictures of those same things. That is lexicography and archaeology, it is on every card above, and you can check all of it.

What it does not do is advance a definitive symbolic interpretation of them. That a letter carries deeper significance beyond its name and its picture is attested, but it is not conclusive. Where the passages above draw a lesson out of a letter, that is reported as what the Talmud teaches, demonstrating that this was a traditional practice. However, the degree to which this extends, and how such interpretations were intended, is a subject of debate and can be misused, leading to significant confusion — and that is not the purpose of this article. Whether the alphabet holds more than its plain sense is a real question, and a genuinely open one, heavily contested among scholars and within the Jewish community itself. This piece is an introduction, and it leaves that question as something the curious reader can search out and discover for themselves.

These are Jewish sources, and they are quoted here as evidence rather than as authority. They show what was taught, when, and something of how it developed — not what a Latter-day Saint reader is obliged to conclude. Many readers may find connections that resonate with Christian and Latter-day Saint doctrine, but they should do so cautiously, making sure the context and history are verifiable to ensure accuracy and to avoid confusion and misunderstanding. And this kind of study takes a lot of research and practice.

Introducing the letters here is not a judgement on their value, nor on how any community understands them. But a witness this old, this specific and this significant is worth taking seriously — which is exactly why it is cited.

And notice the last row, because it lands where this article started. One reading of *samech ayin* is ***simanim aseh*** — "make signs to remember the Torah." **The Talmud's own alphabet lesson says the letters are a device for holding scripture in the memory.** Which is precisely what Psalm 119 is.

---

## Why the Shape Was Chosen

Psalm 119 is about **God's word** — his law, testimonies, precepts, statutes, commandments, judgments, ways. Nearly every one of its 176 verses names it under one of those terms. And the poet chose to say all of it using **every letter, beginning to end**: everything the language can hold, turned toward one subject.

The psalm tells you plainly why:

> "Thy word have I hid in mine heart, that I might not sin against thee." — <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/11/" target="_blank">Psalm 119:11</a>

An acrostic is built to be memorised. This is a poem about keeping scripture inside you, made in a shape that helps you do it.

And there is one more turn the map quietly contains. **The letters in the third row of each card are not the letters in the fifth.** The square script we read was adopted from Aramaic around the fifth century before Christ; the older forms — the ox head, the house plan, the eye — had largely fallen out of use by the time of Christ, so that most people who sang this psalm could no longer read the alphabet it was built on.

**The poem outlasted them.** A psalm about the permanence of God's word survived the very letters it was written with, which is the psalm making its own case:

> "For ever, O LORD, thy word is settled in heaven." — <a href="https://www.blueletterbible.org/kjv/psa/119/89/" target="_blank">Psalm 119:89</a>

---

## Where This Tradition Comes From

Readers who want the longer history — how far back the practice of finding meaning in the Hebrew letters actually goes, and why the claim that it began with medieval Kabbalah does not hold up — will find it laid out with the primary sources in **[More Than Modern: The Ancient Roots of Hebrew Letter Traditions](/study-library/articles/ancient-alphabet-traditions/)**.

---

## Try This

Pick one letter and read only its eight verses. Twenty-two stanzas at a sitting is a great deal; one stanza is a morning.

Twenty-two mornings, and you will have read the longest chapter in the Bible the way it was built to be read: **one letter at a time** — which is, after all, how anybody learns to read.
